I don't have anything else to say about this.\n\nDoctor: When you're alone and not talking to anyone, do you ever feel like your thoughts are being inserted into your mind or that they're not your own in any way?\n\nPatient: I... I thought I just answered that. No. Nobody is putting thoughts in my head. They're my own. I really don't know why you keep asking me the exact same thing.\n\nDoctor: So far you’ve said that your thoughts feel disorganized only when you're talking to people, like customers, and not when you're alone. Is that always the case, or have you ever noticed your thoughts being jumbled or hard to follow even in other situations, like when you’re with friends or family?\n\nPatient: It's mostly just... at the shop. That's why I came in, really. Someone will ask me a simple question, like... when a ring repair will be ready. And my answer just... it starts going from the shop invoices to garden soil, and then to mortgage papers. I don't mean to, but... I can see them losing me. I just get jumbled when people are standing there expecting an answer.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your thoughts were being changed or altered by someone else, even when you weren’t talking to anyone at all?\n\nPatient: No. I... I don't feel that. I already told you, my thoughts are my own. Nobody is changing them.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever noticed your thoughts becoming jumbled or disorganized when you're talking with friends or family, not just customers?\n\nPatient: I don't... I don't really see my family much. And I don't go out with friends. So... no, I guess not. It's just at the shop.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your thoughts were being inserted into your mind or not your own during moments of stress or in any other situation besides talking to customers?\n\nPatient: No. I... I just said that. My thoughts are mine. Nobody is putting things in my head. It's just the jumbling when I have to talk to people at work. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced your thoughts becoming jumbled or disorganized in situations other than talking to customers, such as when you're stressed, alone, or interacting with others outside of work?\n\nPatient: I... no. Like I said, it's really just at the shop. When I'm alone working on a piece, I'm fine. I don't really know what else to tell you.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced your thoughts becoming disorganized or felt like thoughts were being inserted into your mind in any situation, such as when you're under stress or completely alone, even outside of work?\n\nPatient: I... I feel like you keep asking me the same thing. No. When I'm alone, I just focus on the metal, the stones. It's quiet. My thoughts are just... my thoughts. I don't know why you keep asking about that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever noticed your thoughts becoming disorganized or jumbled when you're working alone on jewelry or in other quiet, low-pressure moments?\n\nPatient: No. I... I don't. It's just quiet when I work. I'm just focusing on the pieces. That's it.\n\nDoctor: When you're alone and thinking to yourself, do you ever notice your thoughts becoming jumbled or difficult to follow, even if you're not speaking out loud or answering someone's question?\n\nPatient: I... no. Not when I'm alone. It's... it's just when people ask me things. Like I said before. Someone comes in, they ask a simple question... like when a ring repair will be ready. And I try to answer, but my words... they start going from the invoices, to the garden soil, to the mortgage papers. I can see them looking at me, losing track of what I'm saying. But when I'm alone... no, it's fine.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced your thoughts becoming disorganized or felt like thoughts were being inserted into your mind in any situation — even when you're alone, under stress, or not speaking to anyone?\n\nPatient: I... I don't know how else to say it. You keep asking the same thing. I already told you. When I'm alone, it's fine. It's just... when people talk to me. Like I said. Someone asks about a ring repair, and I try to tell them, but then I'm talking about the invoices, and then the garden soil, and the mortgage... I can see them looking at me like they don't understand. But not when I'm alone. I don't... I don't have thoughts put in my head. I just... I get mixed up when I have to answer people. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced your thoughts becoming disorganized or felt like thoughts were being inserted into your mind in any situation — even when you're alone, under stress, or not speaking to anyone?\n\nPatient: I... I feel like we're going in circles. I'm sorry, I just... I don't know what else you want me to say. I don't have thoughts put into my head. I just... I get mixed up. That's why I came in. People ask a simple question, like about a repair, and... and my answer just goes all over the place. Invoices, the garden, the mortgage... I can see I'm losing them. But it's only when I'm trying to talk to them. Not when I'm alone."
